# Data Kadai story bank: candidates

Made by `scripts/datayt/mine_stories.py` from the tables on this drive: 99 RBI series that add up to a national total, 169 RBI rate or per-person series, 101 NFHS-6 indicators, the mobile-coverage table, and the state-wise tables parsed from Lok Sabha answers (`scripts/datayt/parse_loksabha.py`).

These are leads found by code, not scripts. Tier A is a strong, clear effect on a familiar subject; B is usable; C is weak or carries a caution. A lead becomes a script only after its number is read at the publisher, a newer release is ruled out, and the builder recomputes every comparison.
